MetricInstrumentThresholdResultMet?

293.5 ± 131.8 ms (Physical 6GB RAM, 8 Cores) |

184.6 ± 21.9 ms (Emulator 6GB RAM, 4 Cores)		 Yes
R_peak
(client peak RAM)		 ADB meminfo (TOTAL PSS) ≤ 512 MB Deepseek-V4 (client only):
81.8 MB (Physical 6GB RAM, 8 Cores) |

66.4 MB (Emulator 6GB RAM, 4 Cores)		 Yes
  • L_e2e (≤ 10,000 ms): Nielsen, J. (1993), Usability Engineering / NN/G, Response Times: The 3 Important Limits (10 s limit for keeping user attention focused in dialogue; with offline Whisper batch STT accounting for 4,044.8 ms). Under streaming voice-to-voice targets (≤ 400 ms one-way audio / ~200 ms human turn gap), see ITU-T G.1010, Stivers et al. (2009, PNAS), and Skantze (2021, Computer Speech & Language).
  • L_full (≤ 4,000 ms): ITU-T Recommendation G.1010 (End-user multimedia QoS categories: < 2,000 ms preferred, < 4,000 ms acceptable upper bound for interactive data/query responses).
  • L_tft (≤ 450 ms): MLCommons, MLPerf Inference v5.0 Interactive LLM SLO (≤ 450 ms TTFT for interactive serving); formal TTFT/TPOT SLO disaggregation in Zhong et al. (2024), DistServe (arXiv:2401.09670), well under Nielsen’s 1,000 ms uninterrupted-thought limit.
  • R_peak (≤ 512 MB): Tightened thin-client React Native/Hermes PSS target, well below Google Play’s 6 GB-device foreground P90 ceiling (2.25 GB Anonymous RSS + Swap) in Android Vitals Bad Behavior Thresholds.
